20.662pt}}_{{\kern-23.52498pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 20.662pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle G}}}}\left\{x\penalty\mid\penalty y^{\mathrm{op}}\right\}^{{\kern-22.09953pt{\scriptscriptstyle X}\kern 18.81412pt}}_{{\kern-21.6771pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 18.81412pt}}}=(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH).

Note that
\mathchoice{\left\{\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\penalty\mid\penalty\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\right\}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle H}}}}{\left\{\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\penalty\mid\penalty\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\right\}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle H}}}}{\left\{\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\penalty\mid\penalty\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\right\}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle H}}}}{\left\{\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\penalty\mid\penalty\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\right\}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle H}}}}
indeed lands in
H
since
\rho(x)=\rho(y)
.

Now suppose that
A
is a
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebra with action
\alpha\colon X\to\operatorname{Aut}(A)
.
Let
\mathscr{B}=A\rtimes H
be the Fell bundle over
H
that encodes the restriction
\alpha|_{H}
, meaning that, for each
h\in H
, the fibre
B(h)
is
A
and the structure maps of
\mathscr{B}
are given by

(a_{1},h_{1})\cdot(a_{2},h_{2})\coloneqq(a_{1}\alpha_{h_{1}}(a_{2}),h_{1}h_{2})\quad\text{and}\quad(a,h)^{*}\coloneqq(\alpha_{h^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}}(a)^{*},h^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}).

Let
\mathscr{M}=A\times X
be the “trivial
A
-bundle” over
X
. Then
\mathscr{B}
acts on
\mathscr{M}
via

M\times B\to M,\quad(m,x)\mathchoice{\mathbin{\raisebox{-0.9pt}{$\includegraphics[scale,angle,origin]{semithickHleftB.pdf}$}}}{\mathbin{\raisebox{-0.9pt}{$\includegraphics[scale,angle,origin]{semithickHleftB.pdf}$}}}{\mathbin{\raisebox{-0.6pt}{$\includegraphics[scale,angle,origin]{semithickHleftB.pdf}$}}}{\mathbin{\raisebox{-0.2pt}{$\includegraphics[scale,angle,origin]{semithickHleftB.pdf}$}}}(b,h)\coloneqq(m\alpha_{x}(b),xh),

and we have a
\mathscr{B}
-valued inner product given by

M{\mskip-2.0mu}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}\ast^{{\kern-5.52933pt\kern 5.91821pt}}_{{\kern-7.83887pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}\kern 5.91821pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}\ast^{{\kern-5.52933pt\kern 5.91821pt}}_{{\kern-7.83887pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}\kern 5.91821pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}\ast^{{\kern-3.8821pt\kern 4.27098pt}}_{{\kern-6.19164pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}\kern 4.27098pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}\ast^{{\kern-3.1821pt\kern 3.57098pt}}_{{\kern-5.49164pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}\kern 3.57098pt{\scriptscriptstyle\rho}}}}{\mskip-2.0mu}M\to B,\quad{\mathchoice{\langle(m,x)\penalty\mid\penalty(n,y)\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathscr{B}}}}}{\langle(m,x)\penalty\mid\penalty(n,y)\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathscr{B}}}}}{\langle(m,x)\penalty\mid\penalty(n,y)\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathscr{B}}}}}{\langle(m,x)\penalty\mid\penalty(n,y)\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle\mathscr{B}}}}}}\coloneqq\bigl(\alpha_{x^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}}(m^{*}n),x^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}y\bigr).

One quickly checks that
\mathscr{M}
is a
\mathscr{B}
-demi-equivalence,
so
\mathscr{B}
is equivalent via
\mathscr{M}
to
\mathscr{M}\otimes_{\mathscr{B}}\mathscr{M}^{\mathrm{op}}
. Let us identify the fibre of the latter Fell bundle over an element
(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle G}}}}\left\{x\penalty\mid\penalty y^{\mathrm{op}}\right\}^{{\kern-33.38556pt{\scriptscriptstyle X}\kern 30.10014pt}}_{{\kern-32.96312pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 30.10014pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle G}}}}\left\{x\penalty\mid\penalty y^{\mathrm{op}}\right\}^{{\kern-33.38556pt{\scriptscriptstyle X}\kern 30.10014pt}}_{{\kern-32.96312pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 30.10014pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle G}}}}\left\{x\penalty\mid\penalty y^{\mathrm{op}}\right\}^{{\kern-23.94742pt{\scriptscriptstyle X}\kern 20.662pt}}_{{\kern-23.52498pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 20.662pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\scriptscriptstyle X}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle G}}}}\left\{x\penalty\mid\penalty y^{\mathrm{op}}\right\}^{{\kern-22.09953pt{\scriptscriptstyle X}\kern 18.81412pt}}_{{\kern-21.6771pt{\scriptscriptstyle G}\kern 18.81412pt}}}
of
G
: As explained in Theorem
3.18
, we use (
3.8
) to identify

\displaystyle\left(\mathscr{M}\otimes_{\mathscr{B}}\mathscr{M}^{\mathrm{op}}\right)(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)\cong M(x)\otimes_{e}M(y)^{\mathrm{op}}

as
\compacts_{B(e)}(M(x))-\compacts_{B(e)}(M(y))
-imprimitivity bimodules.
Over a unit
(1_{G},yH)
of
G
, the fibre is (canonically isomorphic to) the
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebra
\compacts_{B(e)}(M(y))
itself, where
y
is any representative of
yH
. These
B(e)
-compact operators on
M(y)
are generated by
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert n_{1}\right\rangle^{{\kern-14.17113pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 14.56001pt}}_{{\kern-14.17113pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 14.56001pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert n_{1}\right\rangle^{{\kern-14.17113pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 14.56001pt}}_{{\kern-14.17113pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 14.56001pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert n_{1}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.46895pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.85783pt}}_{{\kern-11.46895pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.85783pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert n_{1}\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.62862pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.0175pt}}_{{\kern-10.62862pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.0175pt}}}\left\langle n_{2}\right\rvert
for
n_{i}\in M(y)
, and one quickly computes that such a rank-one operator just multiplies
n\in M(y)
on the left by
n_{1}n_{2}^{*}
.

The balancing in
M(x)\otimes_{e}M(y)^{\mathrm{op}}
identifies

(m\alpha_{x}(b),x)\otimes(n,y)^{\mathrm{op}}=(m,x)\otimes(n\alpha_{y}(b^{*}),y)^{\mathrm{op}}

for all
b\in B(e)=A
. If we let
A(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)\coloneqq A
as a Banach space, then we can therefore show that the map

\displaystyle M(x)\otimes_{e}M(y)^{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\to A(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)

\displaystyle(m,x)\otimes(n,y)^{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\mapsto m\alpha_{xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}}(n)^{*}

is a Banach space isomorphism.
These isomorphisms create a new Fell bundle
\mathscr{A}=(p_{\mathscr{A}}\colon A\to G)
out of
\mathscr{M}\otimes_{\mathscr{B}}\mathscr{M}^{\mathrm{op}}
:
\mathscr{A}
has the fibre
A(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)
over
(xy^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},yH)
, and the structure maps of
\mathscr{M}\otimes_{\mathscr{B}}\mathscr{M}^{\mathrm{op}}
described in (
3.24
) and Corollary
3.10
translate to

\displaystyle\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\cdot\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}\colon\qquad

\displaystyle A(x,yzH)\times A(y,zH)\to A(xy,zH),\quad(a,b)\mapsto a\alpha_{x}(b),

and

\displaystyle\makebox[6.99997pt]{\kern 0.70007pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=1.29167pt\kern 0.70007pt}^{*}\colon\qquad

\displaystyle A(x,yH)\to A(x^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}},xyH),\quad a\mapsto\alpha_{x^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}}(a^{*}).

The Fell bundle
\mathscr{A}
is therefore exactly the semi-direct product Fell bundle
X\ltimes(A\times X/H)
which encodes the left-
X
action

x\cdot(a,yH)=(\alpha_{x}(a),xyH)
on the constant
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebraic bundle
A\times X/H
over
X/H
. We have therefore recovered the same Fell bundle that was mentioned as being equivalent to
\mathscr{B}
in
[
20
, Example 14]
.

Example
6.9
is the extreme case where
X
is a group; the other extreme case is when
X
is just a principal
H
-space: We can likewise define a free and proper action of the transformation group groupoid
X\rtimes H
on
X
by letting
(x,h)
transform
x
into
x\mathchoice{\displaystyle\mathbin{\smalltriangleleft}}{\textstyle\mathbin{\smalltriangleleft}}{\scriptstyle\mathbin{\smalltriangleleft}}{\scriptscriptstyle\mathbin{\smalltriangleleft}}h
. The associated imprimitivity groupoid
X\times_{X\rtimes H}X^{\mathrm{op}}
is exactly the quotient
X/H
of the original action on
X
, and so an application of
[
21
, Theorem 2.8]
recovers Green’s theorem that
C_{0}(X/H)
and
C_{0}(X)\rtimes_{r}H
are strongly Morita equivalent.

There is a plethora of generalizations of Green’s result. For example, we can allow the object
X
that is being acted on to be not just a space (as in Green’s theorem) or a group (as in Example
6.9
), but a groupoid
\mathcal{X}
, and we allow the underlying bundles to be more than line-bundles: if
\mathscr{M}=(M\to\mathcal{X})
is a
Fell bundle
that carries a free and proper action of a group
K
, then
\mathscr{M}
is an equivalence between the semi-direct product Fell bundle
\mathscr{B}=\mathscr{M}\rtimes K
and the quotient Fell bundle
K\backslash\mathscr{M}
.

14
14

14

This is a special case of
[
15
, Theorem 3.1]
, where they consider
two
group actions on a groupoid
\mathcal{X}
.
[
5
, Theorem 6.1]
goes two steps further, by firstly allowing groupoid actions, and secondly by allowing the actions to be only
self-similar
rather than by homomorphisms; this yields a Fell bundle equivalence between the Zappa–Szép product Fell bundles
(\mathscr{M}/\mathcal{K}_{1})\bowtie\mathcal{K}_{2}
and
\mathcal{K}_{1}\bowtie(\mathcal{K}_{2}\backslash\mathscr{M})
as constructed in
[
6
]
.
Theorem
1.6
can recover such results, in that
\mathscr{M}
is a
\mathscr{B}
-demi-equivalence, and
\mathscr{M}\otimes_{\mathscr{B}}\mathscr{M}^{\mathrm{op}}
can be computed to be isomorphic to the quotient bundle.

Appendix A
Hilbert
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-Modules

Lemma A.1
.

If
\mathbf{X}
is a full right Hilbert
C^{*}
-module over a
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebra
B
and
\mathbf{x}_{i}\in\mathbf{X}
, then the operator-norm of the
B
-compact positive operator
\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}_{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}_{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.9576pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.34648pt}}_{{\kern-10.9576pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.34648pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.10788pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 10.49677pt}}_{{\kern-10.10788pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 10.49677pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rvert
is exactly the norm of

\sum_{i=1}^{k}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}
in
B
.

Proof.

Denote the compact operator in question by
T
. For any
\mathbf{z}\in\mathbf{X}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum\nolimits_{i}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}=\sum\nolimits_{i}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}.

Note that the right-hand side is a sum of positive elements and hence positive, so
T
is indeed a positive operator by
[
RaWi:Morita
, Lemma 2.28]
.
By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality for Hilbert modules
[
RaWi:Morita
, Lemma 2.5]
, we have for each
i

{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\leq\left\|\mathbf{z}\right\|^{2}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}

in the
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebra
B
, and hence we conclude that

{\mathchoice{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\leq\left\|\mathbf{z}\right\|^{2}\sum\nolimits_{i}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}.

Using
[
RaWi:Morita
, Remark 2.29]
and positivity of
T
in the first equality of the following, we deduce

\displaystyle\left\|T\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup\{\left\|{\mathchoice{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle T\mathbf{z}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\right\|:\left\|\mathbf{z}\right\|\leq 1\}\leq\left\|\sum\nolimits_{i}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\right\|.

For the reverse inequality, note that
\mathbf{X}
is an imprimitivity bimodule between
\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{X})
and
B
, meaning that
B
is exactly the algebra of
\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{X})
-compact operators on the
left
-Hilbert
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-module
\mathbf{X}
. In particular, the same proof for
T
replaced by

\sum_{i}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}
yields the other estimate.
∎

The following result is well known.

Lemma A.2
.

Suppose
\mathbf{X}
is an
A-B
-imprimitivity bimodule and
\mathbf{Y}
is a
C-B
-imprimitivity bimodule. Then the balanced tensor product
\mathbf{X}\otimes_{B}\mathbf{Y}^{\mathrm{op}}
is isomorphic to the
B
-compact operators
\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{X})
as
A-C
-imprimitivity bimodules.

Proof.

First recall that
\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{X})
is densely spanned by the maps

\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert\colon\quad\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\mapsto\mathbf{x}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}

for
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbf{X},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbf{Y}
. Its bimodule structure is given by

a\cdot\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert\coloneqq\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert a\cdot\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-19.59059pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.97948pt}}_{{\kern-19.59059pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.97948pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert a\cdot\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-19.59059pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.97948pt}}_{{\kern-19.59059pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.97948pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert a\cdot\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-13.203pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.59189pt}}_{{\kern-13.203pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.59189pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert a\cdot\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.22435pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.61324pt}}_{{\kern-11.22435pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.61324pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert\quad\text{and}\quad\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert\cdot c\coloneqq\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle c^{*}\cdot\mathbf{y}\right\rvert.

The map

\mathbf{X}\times\mathbf{Y}^{\mathrm{op}}\to\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{X}),\quad(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{\mathrm{op}})\mapsto\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert,

is bilinear, and so it descends to a linear map with domain
\mathbf{X}\odot\mathbf{Y}^{\mathrm{op}}
. For any
b\in B
, we have

(\mathbf{x}\cdot b)\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}=\mathbf{x}\cdot{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}},\text{ so that }\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\cdot b\right\rangle^{{\kern-18.7696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.1585pt}}_{{\kern-18.7696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.1585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\cdot b\right\rangle^{{\kern-18.7696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.1585pt}}_{{\kern-18.7696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 19.1585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\cdot b\right\rangle^{{\kern-12.71582pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.1047pt}}_{{\kern-12.71582pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.1047pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\cdot b\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.87637pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.26526pt}}_{{\kern-10.87637pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.26526pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert=\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*}\right\rvert.

Since
(\mathbf{y}\cdot b^{*})^{\mathrm{op}}=b\cdot\mathbf{y}^{\mathrm{op}}
, we conclude that we have a linear map

\mathbf{X}\odot_{B}\mathbf{Y}^{\mathrm{op}}\to\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{X})\text{ determined by }\mathbf{x}\odot\mathbf{y}^{\mathrm{op}}\mapsto\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}_{{\kern-11.05696pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.44585pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}_{{\kern-9.2518pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 9.64069pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}\right\rangle^{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}_{{\kern-8.40208pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 8.79097pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}\right\rvert.

Clearly, this map is an
A-C
-bimodule map and,
by definition of
\compacts_{B}(\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{X})
, it has dense range. Thus, if we can show that the map is isometric, then it extends to an isomorphism of Hilbert bimodules. For
\mathbf{x}_{i}\in\mathbf{X}
and
\mathbf{y}_{i},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbf{Y}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}_{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}_{{\kern-13.24507pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 13.63396pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.9576pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.34648pt}}_{{\kern-10.9576pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 11.34648pt}}}{\hphantom{}\left\lvert\mathbf{x}_{i}\right\rangle^{{\kern-10.10788pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 10.49677pt}}_{{\kern-10.10788pt{\scriptscriptstyle}\kern 10.49677pt}}}\left\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\right\rvert(\mathbf{y})\right\|_{\mathbf{X}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i,j}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{j}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{j}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{j}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{i}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{i}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{j}\cdot{{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{y}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}\right\|_{B}=\left\|\sum_{i,j}{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{y}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{y}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle , we can write
{\mathchoice{\langle\mathbf{x}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}{\langle\mathbf{x}_{j}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{x}_{i}\rangle^{{{{\mskip-2.0mu}\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{X}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle B}}}}}=\sum_{l}b_{j,l}b_{i,l}^{*}

, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{l}{\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle C}}}}\langle\mathbf{z}_{l}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}_{l}\rangle^{{\kern-26.69328pt{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}\kern 21.9058pt}}_{{\kern-24.76878pt{\scriptscriptstyle C}\kern 21.9058pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle C}}}}\langle\mathbf{z}_{l}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}_{l}\rangle^{{\kern-26.69328pt{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}\kern 21.9058pt}}_{{\kern-24.76878pt{\scriptscriptstyle C}\kern 21.9058pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle C}}}}\langle\mathbf{z}_{l}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}_{l}\rangle^{{\kern-21.42271pt{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}\kern 17.10191pt}}_{{\kern-19.96489pt{\scriptscriptstyle C}\kern 17.10191pt}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}}}_{{{\scriptscriptstyle C}}}}\langle\mathbf{z}_{l}\penalty\mid\penalty\mathbf{z}_{l}\rangle^{{\kern-19.68051pt{\hfill\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{Y}}\kern 15.35971pt}}_{{\kern-18.22269pt{\scriptscriptstyle C}\kern 15.35971pt}}}}\right\|_{C}

compacts}}{=}\left\|\sum_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}\otimes\mathbf{y}_{i}^{\mathrm{op}}\right\|_{\mathbf{X}\otimes_{B}\mathbf{Y}^{\mathrm{op}}}^{2},

as claimed.
∎

Appendix B
upper semi-continuous Banach bundles

The following lemma explains that, in an upper semi-continuous Banach bundle, being arbitrarily close to convergent nets implies convergence. It is, in essence, just a restatement of
[
29
, Proposition C.20]
, where we drop the assumption that the bundle be a
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-bundle.

Lemma B.1
.

Let
\mathscr{M}=(q_{\mathscr{M}}\colon M\to X)
be an upper semi-continuous Banach bundle over a locally compact Hausdorff space
X
. Suppose we are given a net
(m_{\lambda})_{\lambda}
in
\mathscr{M}
and a point
m\in\mathscr{M}
such that
q_{\mathscr{M}}(m_{\lambda})\to q_{\mathscr{M}}(m)
. Then
m_{\lambda}\to m
if and only if, for every
\epsilon>0
, there exists a convergent net
(n_{\lambda}^{\epsilon})_{\lambda}
in
\mathscr{M}
with
q_{\mathscr{M}}(n_{\lambda}^{\epsilon})=q_{\mathscr{M}}(m_{\lambda})
such that for all large
\lambda
, we have

\left\|m_{\lambda}-n_{\lambda}^{\epsilon}\right\|<\epsilon\text{ and }\left\|m-\lim_{\lambda}n_{\lambda}^{\epsilon}\right\|<\epsilon.

Proof.

The forwards implication is trivial. The backwards implication follows from an application of
[
4
, Lemma A.3, (i)
\implies
(ii)]
(applied to the convergent net
(n_{\lambda}^{\epsilon})_{\lambda}
), the triangle inequality, and an application of
[
4
, Lemma A.3, (i)
\impliedby
(ii)]
.
∎

Lemma B.2

(cf.
[
4
, Proposition A.7]
)
.

Let
\mathscr{M}=(q_{\mathscr{M}}\colon M\to X)
and
\mathscr{N}=(q_{\mathscr{N}}\colon N\to Y)
be two upper semi-continuous Banach bundles. Suppose we have a commutative diagram

(B.1)

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathscr{M}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathscr{N}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces X}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces Y}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces q_{\mathscr{M}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\Omega}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces q_{\mathscr{N}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\omega}

and that the maps
satisfy the following conditions.

(a)

for each
x\in X
,
\Omega|_{M_{x}}
is linear;

(b)

there exists a constant
K>0
such that
\|\Omega(m)\|\leq K\|m\|
for all
m\in M
;

(c)

\omega
is a continuous map; and

(d)

there exists a collection
\Gamma
of continuous sections of
\mathscr{M}
such that the
\mathbb{C}
-linear span of

\{\gamma(x):\gamma\in\Gamma\}
is dense in each
M_{x}
and, for each
\gamma\in\Gamma
, the section

\Omega\circ\gamma\colon x\mapsto\bigl(x,\Omega(\gamma(x))\bigr)
of the pull-back bundle
\omega^{*}(\mathscr{N})
is continuous.

Then
\Omega
is continuous.

Proof.

First we point out that the proof of
[
4
, Proposition A.7]
does not require
\Gamma
to be a vector space but only that
\mathrm{span}_{\mathbb{C}}\{\gamma(x):\gamma\in\Gamma\}
is dense in
M(x)
; in other words, we already know that Lemma
B.2
holds in the case that
\omega
is a homeomorphism.

Consider the commutative diagram

(B.2)

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces m}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\bigl(q_{\mathscr{M}}(m),\Omega(m)\bigr)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\Omega(m)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathscr{M}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\omega^{*}(\mathscr{N})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathscr{N}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces X}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces X}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces Y}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\in}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\in}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\in}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces q_{\mathscr{M}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\Psi}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathrm{pr}_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces q_{\mathscr{N}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\omega}

We claim that the map
\Psi
is continuous, which will imply that
\Omega
is continuous since Diagram
B.2
commutes. Since the identity map on
X
is a homeomorphism, we are in good shape to invoke
[
4
, Proposition A.7]
for the square on the left-hand side; because of Assumption
(d)
, it only remains to verify that
\Psi
is fibrewise linear and bounded.
But clearly,
Assumption
(a)

implies linearity of
\Psi|_{M_{x}}\colon M_{x}\to\omega^{*}(\mathscr{N})_{x}=\{x\}\times\mathscr{N}_{\omega(x)}
by the definition of the linear structure on
\omega^{*}(\mathscr{N})
. And likewise, by definition of the norm on
\omega^{*}(\mathscr{N})_{x}
, we have
\left\|\Psi(m)\right\|=\left\|\Omega(m)\right\|
, and hence
\left\|\Psi(m)\right\|\leq K\left\|m\right\|
for all
m\in M
by
(b)
. Using
[
4
, Proposition A.7]
, we conclude that
\Psi
and hence
\Omega
is continuous.
∎

Lemma B.3

(cf.
[
4
, Proposition A.8]
)
.

Let
\mathscr{M}=(q_{\mathscr{M}}\colon M\to X)
and
\mathscr{N}=(q_{\mathscr{N}}\colon N\to Y)
be two upper semi-continuous Banach bundles, and suppose we have the commutative Diagram (
B.1
)
and that the maps
\Omega,\omega

satisfy the following conditions.

(a)

for each
x\in X
,
\Omega|_{M_{x}}
is linear;

(b)

there exists a constant
k>0
such that
\|\Omega(m)\|\geq k\|m\|
for all
m\in M
;

(c)

\omega
is an embedding (i.e., a homeomorphism onto its image); and

(d)

\Omega
is continuous.

Then
\Omega^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}\colon\Omega(M)\to M
is continuous.

Proof.

As in the proof of Lemma
B.2
, consider the map
\Psi
in Diagram (
B.2
). Again, since
\Omega|_{M_{x}}
is linear, so is
\Psi|_{M_{x}}
, and since
\Omega
is bounded below, so is
\Psi
. This time, continuity of
\Omega
implies continuity of
\Psi
. We can therefore apply
[
4
, Proposition A.8]
to the square on the left-hand side of Diagram (
B.2
) to conclude that the map
\Psi^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}\colon\Psi(M)\to M
is continuous.

Since
q_{\mathscr{N}}(\Omega(m))=\omega(q_{\mathscr{M}}(m))
by commutativity of Diagram (
B.1
), it follows from Assumption
(c)
that the map
f=q_{\mathscr{M}}\circ\omega^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}\circ q_{\mathscr{N}}\colon\Omega(M)\to X,\Omega(m)\mapsto q_{\mathscr{M}}(m)
, is well defined and continuous.
By definition of
\Psi
, we have

\bigl(f(\Omega(m)),\Omega(m)\bigr)=\bigl(q_{\mathscr{M}}(m),\Omega(m)\bigr)=\Psi(m),

so that for
n\in\Omega(M)
,

\Psi^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}(f(n),n)=\Omega^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}(n)
. It follows that
\Omega^{\scalebox{.7}{$\scriptstyle-1$}}
is continuous as concatenation of continuous maps.
∎
